     id: 9,
     key: 'electronBuild',
     name: 'Electron build OK — declaration-level packaging readiness (CI is install-free by policy)',
     run(io) {
       const problems = [];
       let pkg;
       try { pkg = io.readJson(path.join(REPO, 'package.json')); } catch (e) {
         return { ok: false, detail: { problems: ['package.json unreadable: ' + e.message] } };
       }
       // 9a. devDependencies are exactly the two build tools with well-formed ranges.
       const dev = pkg.devDependencies || {};
       const devKeys = Object.keys(dev).sort();
       if (devKeys.join(',') !== 'electron,electron-builder') problems.push('devDependencies must be exactly {electron, electron-builder}; got ' + devKeys.join(','));
       for (const k of devKeys) {
         if (!/^[\^~]?\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/.test(String(dev[k]))) problems.push('devDependency ' + k + ' has a non-simple range: ' + dev[k]);
       }
       if (pkg.dependencies && Object.keys(pkg.dependencies).length > 0) problems.push('runtime dependencies must be empty; got ' + Object.keys(pkg.dependencies).join(','));
       // 9b. build block shape.
       const b = pkg.build || {};
       if (b.appId !== 'com.racingsetup.analyzer') problems.push('build.appId drifted: ' + b.appId);
       if (b.productName !== 'Racing Setup Analyzer') problems.push('build.productName drifted: ' + b.productName);
       if (!b.directories || typeof b.directories.output !== 'string' || b.directories.output.length === 0) problems.push('build.directories.output missing');
       // 9c. files allowlist is exactly the three production roots.
       const files = Array.isArray(b.files) ? b.files.slice().sort() : [];
       const expected = ['main.js', 'preload.js', 'renderer/**/*'].sort();
       if (JSON.stringify(files) !== JSON.stringify(expected)) problems.push('build.files must be exactly [main.js, preload.js, renderer/**/*]; got ' + JSON.stringify(b.files));
       // 9d. every asset the build config references must exist on disk (no dangling icons).
       for (const [plat, cfg] of [['mac', b.mac], ['win', b.win]]) {
         if (cfg && typeof cfg.icon === 'string') {
           if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(REPO, cfg.icon))) problems.push('build.' + plat + '.icon references a missing file: ' + cfg.icon);
         }
       }
       // 9e. the three packaged roots exist.
       for (const f of ['main.js', 'preload.js', 'renderer/index.html']) {
         if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(REPO, f))) problems.push('packaged root missing: ' + f);
       }
       // 9f. renderer tree hygiene: nothing that must not ship.
       const rendererDir = path.join(REPO, 'renderer');
       const bad = [];
       (function walk(dir) {
         for (const name of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
           const full = path.join(dir, name);
           const st = fs.statSync(full);
           if (st.isDirectory()) {
             if (name === 'node_modules') { bad.push(path.relative(REPO, full)); continue; }
             walk(full);
           } else if (name === '.DS_Store' || name.endsWith('.map') || name.endsWith('.env')) {
             bad.push(path.relative(REPO, full));
           }
         }
       })(rendererDir);
       if (bad.length) problems.push('renderer tree contains non-shippable entries: ' + bad.join(', '));
       // 9g. every script/lib the page references exists on disk (missing-asset guard).
       const html = fs.readFileSync(path.join(REPO, 'renderer', 'index.html'), 'utf8');
       const srcs = [];
       const re = /<script[^>]+src="([^"]+)"/g;
       let m;
       while ((m = re.exec(html)) !== null) srcs.push(m[1]);
       const missing = srcs.filter((s) => !fs.existsSync(path.join(REPO, 'renderer', s)));
       if (srcs.length < 90) problems.push('suspiciously few <script src> tags parsed from renderer/index.html: ' + srcs.length);
       if (missing.length) problems.push('renderer/index.html references missing assets: ' + missing.join(', '));
       return { ok: problems.length === 0, detail: { problems: problems, scriptTags: srcs.length } };
     },
   },
   {
     id: 10,
     key: 'releaseNotes',
     name: 'Release notes drafted — docs/release-notes-2.0.0.md with required sections',
     run(io) {

-      if (!/pinned at 1\.4\.0/i.test(text)) problems.push('missing the pinned-at-1.4.0 statement');
+      if (!/^## \[(Unreleased\] — 2\.0\.0 candidate|2\.0\.0\] — Release Candidate)/m.test(text)) problems.push('missing the 2.0.0 candidate/Release-Candidate section heading');
+      if (!/pinned at .?1\.4\.0/i.test(text)) problems.push('missing the pinned-at-1.4.0 history statement');
       return { ok: problems.length === 0, detail: { problems: problems } };
     },
   },
   {
     id: 12,
     key: 'tagPolicy',
-    name: 'Tag policy — version pinned at 1.4.0, lockfile untracked, workflows install-free',
+    name: 'Tag policy — version pinned at the authorized value (check-version-policy EXPECTED), lockfile untracked, workflows install-free',
     run(io) {
       const r = _corroborate(io, io.delegate('scripts/check-version-policy.js'), 'version-policy.json');
       return { ok: r.ok, detail: r.detail };
     },
   },
 ];
 
 // ── runner ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 
 // io is injectable so tests can exercise each condition's fail-closed judgement
 // without spawning the real (multi-minute) validators.
 function runGate(io) {
   io = io || { delegate: _delegate, readJson: _readJson };
   const results = [];
   for (const cond of CONDITIONS) {
     let out;
     try {
       out = cond.run(io);
     } catch (e) {
       out = { ok: false, detail: { crashed: String((e && e.message) || e) } };
     }
     results.push({ id: cond.id, key: cond.key, name: cond.name, ok: out.ok === true, detail: out.detail });
   }
   const failed = results.filter((r) => !r.ok);
   return {
     schemaVersion: 1,
     gate: 'F5_RELEASE_GATE',
     conditions: results,
     passedCount: results.length - failed.length,
     failedCount: failed.length,
     failedKeys: failed.map((r) => r.key),
     ok: failed.length === 0,
   };
 }
 
 module.exports = { CONDITIONS, runGate, _delegate: _delegate, _readJson: _readJson };
 
 if (require.main === module) {
   let exitCode = 2;
   try {
     fs.mkdirSync(ARTIFACT_DIR, { recursive: true });
     const report = runGate();
     fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ARTIFACT_DIR, 'release-gate.json'), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n');
     process.stdout.write('RELEASE-GATE ' + JSON.stringify({
       ok: report.ok, passed: report.passedCount, failed: report.failedCount, failedKeys: report.failedKeys,
     }) + '\n');
     exitCode = report.ok ? 0 : 1;
   } catch (e) {
     process.stderr.write('RELEASE-GATE FATAL ' + String((e && e.stack) || e) + '\n');
     exitCode = 2;
   }
   process.exit(exitCode);
 }

 #!/usr/bin/env node
 'use strict';
 /**
  * R3-GATE0 — package + version policy check (dependency-free lane).
  *
- * package.json version must remain 1.4.0 (unless VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW=<x.y.z>). The lockfile must stay
+ * package.json version must remain 2.0.0 — the F6_RELEASE pin after the single authorized
+ * 1.4.0 -> 2.0.0 bump (unless VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW=<x.y.z> stages a future authorized bump). The lockfile must stay
  * untracked and must not be introduced by this PR. The CI workflow must contain no package-manager
  * install, Electron build, or lifecycle invocation — R3-GATE0 must not quietly change the project's
  * existing dependency governance. CI never publishes / tags / releases (no release step exists).
  *
  * Env: BASE_SHA / HEAD_SHA (CI supplies; fallback origin/main...HEAD), VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW (optional).
  * Output: ${ARTIFACT_DIR:-artifacts}/version-policy.json   (exit non-zero on any violation)
  */
 const fs = require('fs');
 const path = require('path');
 const cp = require('child_process');
 
 const REPO = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
 const ARTIFACT_DIR = process.env.ARTIFACT_DIR ? path.resolve(process.env.ARTIFACT_DIR) : path.join(REPO, 'artifacts');
-const EXPECTED = '1.4.0';
+const EXPECTED = '2.0.0';
 
 function git(args) {
   const r = cp.spawnSync('git', args, { cwd: REPO, encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 32 * 1024 * 1024 });
   return r.status === 0 ? (r.stdout || '').trim() : null;
 }
 
 function run() {
   const pkg = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(REPO, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
   const allow = (process.env.VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW || '').trim();
   const version = pkg.version;
   const versionOk = version === EXPECTED || (!!allow && version === allow);
 
   const lockfileTracked = !!git(['ls-files', 'package-lock.json']);
   const base = process.env.BASE_SHA || 'origin/main', head = process.env.HEAD_SHA || 'HEAD';
   const changed = (git(['diff', '--name-only', base + '...' + head]) || '').split('\n').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
   const lockfileAddedByPR = changed.includes('package-lock.json');
 
   // The CI workflow must not install packages / build Electron / run lifecycle scripts.
   const wfDir = path.join(REPO, '.github', 'workflows');
   let wfFiles = [];
   try { wfFiles = fs.readdirSync(wfDir).filter(f => /\.ya?ml$/.test(f)); } catch (_) { /* none */ }
   const forbidden = [
     { re: /npm\s+ci\b/, name: 'npm ci' }, { re: /npm\s+install\b/, name: 'npm install' },
     { re: /\bnpx\b/, name: 'npx' }, { re: /\byarn\b/, name: 'yarn' }, { re: /\bpnpm\b/, name: 'pnpm' },
     { re: /electron-builder/, name: 'electron-builder' }, { re: /npm\s+run\s+build/, name: 'npm run build' },
     { re: /build:(mac|win|all)\b/, name: 'electron build script' },
   ];
   const workflowViolations = [];
   for (const f of wfFiles) {
     const txt = fs.readFileSync(path.join(wfDir, f), 'utf8');
     for (const x of forbidden) if (x.re.test(txt)) workflowViolations.push(f + ': ' + x.name);
   }
 
   const ok = versionOk && !lockfileTracked && !lockfileAddedByPR && workflowViolations.length === 0;
   return {
     check: 'version-policy',
     packageVersion: version, expected: EXPECTED, bumpAllow: allow || null, versionOk,
     lockfileTracked, lockfileAddedByPR, workflowViolations, ok,
   };
 }
 
 let result, exitCode;
 try { result = run(); exitCode = result.ok ? 0 : 1; }
 catch (e) { result = { check: 'version-policy', fatalError: String((e && e.stack) || e), packageVersion: null, ok: false }; exitCode = 2; }
 fs.mkdirSync(ARTIFACT_DIR, { recursive: true });
 fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ARTIFACT_DIR, 'version-policy.json'), JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
 console.log('VERSION ' + JSON.stringify({ packageVersion: result.packageVersion, lockfileTracked: result.lockfileTracked, workflowViolations: result.workflowViolations, ok: result.ok }));
 process.exit(exitCode);

 let pass = 0, fail = 0;
 function chk(name, cond, detail) {
   if (cond) pass++;
   else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL ' + name + (detail !== undefined ? '  ' + (typeof detail === 'string' ? detail : JSON.stringify(detail)) : '')); }
 }
 
 function runValidator(base) {
   const tmp = H.acquireTempDir('r3-train-art-');
   const env = Object.assign({}, process.env, { ARTIFACT_DIR: tmp });
   if (base) env.R3_TRAIN_BASE_OVERRIDE = base;
   const r = cp.spawnSync('node', [SCRIPT], { cwd: REPO, encoding: 'utf8', env });
   let artifact = null;
   try { artifact = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(tmp, 'r3-0-train.json'), 'utf8')); } catch (_) { artifact = null; }
   return { status: r.status, artifact, stderr: r.stderr || '', stdout: r.stdout || '', tmp };
 }
 
 function hasViolation(artifact, code) {
   return !!(artifact && Array.isArray(artifact.violations) && artifact.violations.some(v => v.code === code));
 }

 (function () {
   // R3.0C-specific assertions on the train state. After R3.0D D1 advances the train, train.currentPhase
   // moves to R3.0D and train.currentPhaseCheckpoint becomes the active R3.0D checkpoint. R3.0C state
   // however remains pinned at C8_ACTIVATION / finalActivationReached=true (R3.0C is complete). The
   // tests below assert what is INVARIANT — R3.0C is done — without false-failing when R3.0D advances.
   const train = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(REPO, 'governance', 'r3.0', 'train.json'), 'utf8'));
   const r3c = train.phaseStates && train.phaseStates['R3.0C'];
   chk('train.phaseStates.R3.0C.currentCheckpoint=C8_ACTIVATION', r3c && r3c.currentCheckpoint === 'C8_ACTIVATION');
   chk('train.phaseStates.R3.0C.finalActivationReached=true', r3c && r3c.finalActivationReached === true);
   chk('train.phaseStates.R3.0C.finalActivationCheckpoint=C8_ACTIVATION', r3c && r3c.finalActivationCheckpoint === 'C8_ACTIVATION');
   chk('train.currentPhase=R3.0C OR train has advanced to a later phase', train.currentPhase === 'R3.0C' || ['R3.0D', 'R3.0E', 'R3.0F'].indexOf(train.currentPhase) !== -1);
   // R3.0F MUST NOT have reached its final activation (F6_RELEASE) before R3.0C C8_ACTIVATION;
   // it MAY be started (R3.0F.started=true) once R3.0E E5_ACTIVATION finalActivationReached=true,
   // mirroring the precedent that allowed R3.0D to start after R3.0C C8 and R3.0E to start after
   // R3.0D D5. Per the SKYLINE-pinned scope (no intermediate release), F6_RELEASE itself is the
   // single boundary that authorizes the version bump and the post-merge tag.
   ['R3.0F'].forEach(function (p) {
     const s = train.phaseStates && train.phaseStates[p];
     chk('train.phaseStates.' + p + '.started is boolean', s && (s.started === true || s.started === false));
-    chk('train.phaseStates.' + p + '.finalActivationReached=false', s && s.finalActivationReached === false);
+    // Pre-F6 this had to be false (no premature final activation before C8). At F6_RELEASE
+    // the flag legitimately flips true — but ONLY together with currentCheckpoint=F6_RELEASE,
+    // so the anti-skip property this assertion has always protected stays intact.
+    chk('train.phaseStates.' + p + '.finalActivationReached consistent with its checkpoint',
+      s && (s.finalActivationReached === false
+        || (s.finalActivationReached === true && s.currentCheckpoint === 'F6_RELEASE')));
   });
   // R3.0E is started; after E5_ACTIVATION the finalActivationReached flag may be
   // either false (in-progress) or true (R3.0E phase complete). Both are valid Train
   // intermediate states. The R3.0F phase-state check below remains the source of
   // truth for whether the Integrated Delivery Train has reached its final stage.
   (function () {
     const s = train.phaseStates && train.phaseStates['R3.0E'];
     chk('train.phaseStates.R3.0E exists', s !== undefined);
     chk('train.phaseStates.R3.0E.finalActivationReached is boolean',
       s && (s.finalActivationReached === true || s.finalActivationReached === false));
   })();
 })();

+'use strict';
+/**
+ * tests/version-bump-policy.test.js — R3.0F F6 · version-bump fail-closed contract.
+ *
+ * Guards the single authorized 1.4.0 -> 2.0.0 bump semantics of
+ * scripts/check-version-policy.js by exercising the REAL script in a child process
+ * against a sandboxed repo fixture (a temp dir with a git repo + package.json), so
+ * every case runs the actual policy logic — no reimplementation.
+ *
+ * Covered (per the F6 release-preparation contract):
+ *   - the post-bump pin: version 2.0.0 passes with NO env
+ *   - fail-closed without allow: any OTHER version fails when VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW unset
+ *   - exact-allow-only: allow=3.0.0 accepts exactly 3.0.0, still rejects 2.0.1
+ *   - no rollback: 1.4.0 (the pre-bump value) now FAILS without an explicit allow
+ *   - version scripts create no tag and never enable release_executed
+ */
+const fs = require('fs');
+const path = require('path');
+const cp = require('child_process');
+const H = require('./helpers/managed-temp-dir.js');
+
+let passed = 0, failed = 0;
+function chk(name, cond, extra) {
+  if (cond) { passed++; console.log('  ✓ ' + name); }
+  else { failed++; console.log('  ✗ ' + name + (extra !== undefined ? ' :: ' + JSON.stringify(extra) : '')); }
+}
+
+const REPO = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const SCRIPT = path.join(REPO, 'scripts', 'check-version-policy.js');
+
+// Sandbox: a minimal git repo so the script's git calls behave; its package.json is
+// the variable under test. The script resolves REPO relative to its own location, so
+// we copy the script into the sandbox's scripts/ dir to point it at the fixture.
+function runPolicy(version, env) {
+  const box = H.acquireTempDir('f6-version-policy-');
+  try {
+    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(box, 'scripts'), { recursive: true });
+    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(box, '.github', 'workflows'), { recursive: true });
+    fs.copyFileSync(SCRIPT, path.join(box, 'scripts', 'check-version-policy.js'));
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(box, 'package.json'), JSON.stringify({ name: 'fixture', version: version }) + '\n');
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(box, '.github', 'workflows', 'ci.yml'), 'name: x\non:\n  push:\n');
+    cp.execSync('git init -q && git add -A && git -c user.email=t@t -c user.name=t commit -qm x', { cwd: box });
+    const r = cp.spawnSync(process.execPath, [path.join(box, 'scripts', 'check-version-policy.js')], {
+      cwd: box, encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 30000,
+      env: Object.assign({}, process.env, { ARTIFACT_DIR: path.join(box, 'artifacts'), BASE_SHA: 'HEAD', HEAD_SHA: 'HEAD' }, env || {}),
+    });
+    let artifact = null;
+    try { artifact = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(box, 'artifacts', 'version-policy.json'), 'utf8')); } catch (_) { }
+    return { status: r.status, artifact: artifact };
+  } finally {
+    H.releaseTempDir(box);
+  }
+}
+
+// 1. The post-bump pin: 2.0.0 passes with no allow env.
+(function () {
+  const r = runPolicy('2.0.0', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '' });
+  chk('pin: version 2.0.0 passes with NO VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW', r.status === 0 && r.artifact && r.artifact.ok === true, r.artifact);
+})();
+
+// 2. Fail-closed without allow: a foreign version fails.
+(function () {
+  const r = runPolicy('2.0.1', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '' });
+  chk('fail-closed: 2.0.1 REJECTED without allow', r.status !== 0 && r.artifact && r.artifact.ok === false);
+  const r2 = runPolicy('2.1.0', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '' });
+  chk('fail-closed: 2.1.0 REJECTED without allow', r2.status !== 0);
+  const r3 = runPolicy('2.0.0-rc.1', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '' });
+  chk('fail-closed: 2.0.0-rc.1 REJECTED without allow', r3.status !== 0);
+})();
+
+// 3. No rollback: the PRE-bump value 1.4.0 now fails without an explicit allow.
+(function () {
+  const r = runPolicy('1.4.0', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '' });
+  chk('no-rollback: 1.4.0 REJECTED after the F6 pin moved to 2.0.0', r.status !== 0 && r.artifact && r.artifact.ok === false);
+})();
+
+// 4. Exact-allow-only: an allow admits exactly that version and nothing else.
+(function () {
+  const r = runPolicy('3.0.0', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '3.0.0' });
+  chk('allow: future authorized bump 3.0.0 passes ONLY with exact allow', r.status === 0);
+  const r2 = runPolicy('2.0.1', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '3.0.0' });
+  chk('allow: 2.0.1 still REJECTED when allow=3.0.0 (exact match only)', r2.status !== 0);
+  const r3 = runPolicy('3.0.1', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '3.0.0' });
+  chk('allow: 3.0.1 REJECTED when allow=3.0.0 (no prefix/range semantics)', r3.status !== 0);
+})();
+
+// 5. No re-bump / double-bump semantics: allow does not stack — with allow set to the
+//    current pin the pin itself still passes, but an unrelated jump does not.
+(function () {
+  const r = runPolicy('4.0.0', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '2.0.0' });
+  chk('no-double-bump: 4.0.0 REJECTED even with allow set to the current pin', r.status !== 0);
+})();
+
+// 6. Version tooling creates no tag and never enables release_executed.
+(function () {
+  const src = fs.readFileSync(SCRIPT, 'utf8');
+  chk('version script never creates/pushes a tag', !/git\s+(tag|push)/.test(src));
+  chk('version script never touches release_executed', src.indexOf('release_executed') === -1);
+  const gate = fs.readFileSync(path.join(REPO, 'scripts', 'check-release-gate.js'), 'utf8');
+  chk('release gate never creates/pushes a tag', !/git\s+(tag|push)/.test(gate));
+  chk('release gate never writes governance state', gate.indexOf("writeFileSync(path.join(ARTIFACT_DIR") !== -1 && !/writeFileSync\([^)]*governance/.test(gate));
+  const state = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(REPO, 'governance', 'r3.0f', 'state.json'), 'utf8'));
+  chk('release_executed remains DISABLED at RELEASE_PREPARED', state.enabledCapabilities.indexOf('release_executed') === -1);
+  const pkg = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(REPO, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
+  chk('repo package.json carries exactly the bumped version 2.0.0', pkg.version === '2.0.0');
+})();
+
+console.log('version-bump-policy: ' + passed + ' passed, ' + failed + ' failed');
+if (failed) process.exit(1);

     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	'use strict';
     3	/**
     4	 * R3-GATE0 — package + version policy check (dependency-free lane).
     5	 *
     6	 * package.json version must remain 2.0.0 — the F6_RELEASE pin after the single authorized
     7	 * 1.4.0 -> 2.0.0 bump (unless VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW=<x.y.z> stages a future authorized bump). The lockfile must stay
     8	 * untracked and must not be introduced by this PR. The CI workflow must contain no package-manager
     9	 * install, Electron build, or lifecycle invocation — R3-GATE0 must not quietly change the project's
    10	 * existing dependency governance. CI never publishes / tags / releases (no release step exists).
    11	 *
    12	 * Env: BASE_SHA / HEAD_SHA (CI supplies; fallback origin/main...HEAD), VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW (optional).
    13	 * Output: ${ARTIFACT_DIR:-artifacts}/version-policy.json   (exit non-zero on any violation)
    14	 */
    15	const fs = require('fs');
    16	const path = require('path');
    17	const cp = require('child_process');
    18	
    19	const REPO = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
    20	const ARTIFACT_DIR = process.env.ARTIFACT_DIR ? path.resolve(process.env.ARTIFACT_DIR) : path.join(REPO, 'artifacts');
    21	const EXPECTED = '2.0.0';
    22	
    23	function git(args) {
    24	  const r = cp.spawnSync('git', args, { cwd: REPO, encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 32 * 1024 * 1024 });
    25	  return r.status === 0 ? (r.stdout || '').trim() : null;
    26	}
    27	
    28	function run() {
    29	  const pkg = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(REPO, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
    30	  const allow = (process.env.VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW || '').trim();
    31	  const version = pkg.version;
    32	  const versionOk = version === EXPECTED || (!!allow && version === allow);
    33	
    34	  const lockfileTracked = !!git(['ls-files', 'package-lock.json']);
    35	  const base = process.env.BASE_SHA || 'origin/main', head = process.env.HEAD_SHA || 'HEAD';
    36	  const changed = (git(['diff', '--name-only', base + '...' + head]) || '').split('\n').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    37	  const lockfileAddedByPR = changed.includes('package-lock.json');
    38	
    39	  // The CI workflow must not install packages / build Electron / run lifecycle scripts.
    40	  const wfDir = path.join(REPO, '.github', 'workflows');
    41	  let wfFiles = [];
    42	  try { wfFiles = fs.readdirSync(wfDir).filter(f => /\.ya?ml$/.test(f)); } catch (_) { /* none */ }
    43	  const forbidden = [
    44	    { re: /npm\s+ci\b/, name: 'npm ci' }, { re: /npm\s+install\b/, name: 'npm install' },
    45	    { re: /\bnpx\b/, name: 'npx' }, { re: /\byarn\b/, name: 'yarn' }, { re: /\bpnpm\b/, name: 'pnpm' },
    46	    { re: /electron-builder/, name: 'electron-builder' }, { re: /npm\s+run\s+build/, name: 'npm run build' },
    47	    { re: /build:(mac|win|all)\b/, name: 'electron build script' },
    48	  ];
    49	  const workflowViolations = [];
    50	  for (const f of wfFiles) {
    51	    const txt = fs.readFileSync(path.join(wfDir, f), 'utf8');
    52	    for (const x of forbidden) if (x.re.test(txt)) workflowViolations.push(f + ': ' + x.name);
    53	  }
    54	
    55	  const ok = versionOk && !lockfileTracked && !lockfileAddedByPR && workflowViolations.length === 0;
    56	  return {
    57	    check: 'version-policy',
    58	    packageVersion: version, expected: EXPECTED, bumpAllow: allow || null, versionOk,
    59	    lockfileTracked, lockfileAddedByPR, workflowViolations, ok,
    60	  };
    61	}
    62	
    63	let result, exitCode;
    64	try { result = run(); exitCode = result.ok ? 0 : 1; }
    65	catch (e) { result = { check: 'version-policy', fatalError: String((e && e.stack) || e), packageVersion: null, ok: false }; exitCode = 2; }
    66	fs.mkdirSync(ARTIFACT_DIR, { recursive: true });
    67	fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ARTIFACT_DIR, 'version-policy.json'), JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
    68	console.log('VERSION ' + JSON.stringify({ packageVersion: result.packageVersion, lockfileTracked: result.lockfileTracked, workflowViolations: result.workflowViolations, ok: result.ok }));
    69	process.exit(exitCode);

   367	
   368	// io is injectable so tests can exercise each condition's fail-closed judgement
   369	// without spawning the real (multi-minute) validators.
   370	function runGate(io) {
   371	  io = io || { delegate: _delegate, readJson: _readJson };
   372	  const results = [];
   373	  for (const cond of CONDITIONS) {
   374	    let out;
   375	    try {
   376	      out = cond.run(io);
   377	    } catch (e) {
   378	      out = { ok: false, detail: { crashed: String((e && e.message) || e) } };
   379	    }
   380	    results.push({ id: cond.id, key: cond.key, name: cond.name, ok: out.ok === true, detail: out.detail });
   381	  }
   382	  const failed = results.filter((r) => !r.ok);
   383	  return {
   384	    schemaVersion: 1,
   385	    gate: 'F5_RELEASE_GATE',
   386	    conditions: results,
   387	    passedCount: results.length - failed.length,
   388	    failedCount: failed.length,
   389	    failedKeys: failed.map((r) => r.key),
   390	    ok: failed.length === 0,
   391	  };
   392	}
   393	
   394	module.exports = { CONDITIONS, runGate, _delegate: _delegate, _readJson: _readJson };
   395	
   396	if (require.main === module) {
   397	  let exitCode = 2;
   398	  try {
   399	    fs.mkdirSync(ARTIFACT_DIR, { recursive: true });
   400	    const report = runGate();
   401	    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ARTIFACT_DIR, 'release-gate.json'), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n');
   402	    process.stdout.write('RELEASE-GATE ' + JSON.stringify({
   403	      ok: report.ok, passed: report.passedCount, failed: report.failedCount, failedKeys: report.failedKeys,
   404	    }) + '\n');
   405	    exitCode = report.ok ? 0 : 1;
   406	  } catch (e) {
   407	    process.stderr.write('RELEASE-GATE FATAL ' + String((e && e.stack) || e) + '\n');
   408	    exitCode = 2;
   409	  }
   410	  process.exit(exitCode);
   411	}
     1	# Release Notes — 2.0.0 (Release Candidate)
     2	
     3	> **Status: RELEASE CANDIDATE — prepared for v2.0.0; publication pending explicit
     4	> authorization.** The R3.0 Integrated Delivery Train has been merged to `main`
     5	> (merge commit `0711e74a`) and the single authorized `package.json` version bump
     6	> 1.4.0 → 2.0.0 is staged on the F6 release branch. The `v2.0.0` tag, the GitHub
     7	> Release, and any binary distribution have **NOT** been created — each requires its
     8	> own explicit user authorization. Nothing in this document implies the release has
     9	> been published.
    10	
    11	## What 2.0.0 is
    12	
    13	2.0.0 is the R3.0 Integrated Delivery Train: everything from R3.0A through R3.0F merged
    14	as one release on top of the 1.4.0 baseline. The authoritative per-milestone record is
    15	[CHANGELOG.md](../CHANGELOG.md) (its `[Unreleased] — 2.0.0 candidate` section is the
    16	canonical summary and is intentionally not duplicated here).
    17	
    18	Highlights, briefly:
    19	
    20	- **Case-centric workspace (R3.0A/B)** — persistent Case Library over IndexedDB with a
    21	  frozen portable case-record schema (v1.4.0), export/import bundles, session storage.
    22	- **Comparison foundation (R3.0C)** — same-case + same-session comparison authority,
    23	  explicit-user-selection reference laps, six allowlisted delta metrics with the fixed
    24	  `comparison − reference` sign convention, bounded comparison export. The Comparisons
    25	  pane is live in the app.
    26	- **Decision engine (R3.0D)** — evidence graph → hypothesis → priority → Engineer Brief
    27	  producer chain, authoritative-only inputs (closure-private WeakSet verification),
    28	  advisory-only output in physical units, no runtime LLM, no causation claims, no driver
    29	  blame.
    30	- **Experiment loop (R3.0E)** — experiment / outcome / follow-up-link / timeline stores
    31	  and contracts, append-only timeline, fail-closed outcome classification.
    32	- **Hardening, migration & E2E (R3.0F)** — per-store migration engine (tooling layer),
    33	  nine Node-level E2E flows, six adversarial hardening probes (133 assertions),
    34	  documentation set, and the 12-condition release gate.
    35	
    36	## Installation status: unsigned and unnotarized
    37	
    38	**The macOS build is not code-signed and not notarized. The Windows build is not
    39	code-signed.** This is a deliberate, documented state for 2.0.0:
    40	
    41	- On **macOS**, Gatekeeper will block a plain double-click on first launch. Open via
    42	  right-click → Open (or `System Settings → Privacy & Security → Open Anyway`). The DMG
    43	  carries the default Electron icon — no custom app icon ships in 2.0.0.
    44	- On **Windows**, SmartScreen will warn about an unrecognized app. "More info → Run
    45	  anyway" is required on first launch.
    46	- No auto-update channel exists. The app never contacts an update server (see
    47	  `docs/r3-data-and-privacy.md`, "Network egress").
    48	
    49	## Upgrade guidance (from 1.4.0)
    50	
    51	- 2.0.0 preserves the frozen R3.0B portable case-record schema **v1.4.0 unchanged** —
    52	  existing case data in IndexedDB is read as-is; the runtime upgrade path is the
    53	  per-read migration in `renderer/js/case-store.js` (`migrateCaseRecord` on `open`),
    54	  which currently passes v1 records through unchanged and fail-closes on
    55	  future-version records (`CANNOT_OVERWRITE_FUTURE` guards on every write path).
    56	- The R3.0F F1 migration engine (`renderer/js/r3-0f-migration-engine.js`) ships in the
    57	  package but is **not loaded by the renderer page** — it is exercised at the
    58	  Node-test/tooling layer only in 2.0.0 (see "Known limitations").
    59	- Records written by a FUTURE schema version are rejected fail-closed, never coerced.
    60	- There is no downgrade path: once a future version writes data, 2.0.0 will refuse it.
    61	
    62	## Backup and rollback guidance
    63	
    64	- All persistent state lives in the Electron `userData` profile (IndexedDB) plus one
    65	  `localStorage` key (`lang`). Backing up the `userData` folder while the app is closed
    66	  is a complete backup; restoring it is a complete restore.
    67	- The app itself provides case-level export (`Export` button → JSON bundle download)
    68	  as a portable, partial backup; comparison summaries export separately. Neither export
    69	  contains raw telemetry samples (see `docs/r3-data-and-privacy.md`).
    70	- Rollback = reinstall the previous app version + restore the backed-up `userData`
    71	  folder. In-app data deletion is partial by design (outcome/timeline stores are
    72	  write-once/append-only); only deleting `userData` wipes everything.
    73	
    74	## Privacy
    75	
    76	Local-first; no designed cloud sync; no active product telemetry; no account; no remote
    77	identity. Data leaves the machine only through user-initiated export downloads. The app
    78	cannot control OS-level backups or other local processes. Full statement:
    79	`docs/r3-data-and-privacy.md`.
    80	
    81	## Known limitations (2.0.0)
    82	
    83	1. **Pane wiring is partial.** The Comparisons pane is live and usable. The Engineer
    84	   Brief, Experiment Loop, and Case Timeline **nav nodes** are live in navigation, but
    85	   their pane content is not wired into `renderer/index.html` (the D5 Engineer Brief
    86	   mount ships hidden/inert; the page loads no R3.0E viewmodel script). The underlying
    87	   services and contracts ship, run under Node-level tests, and are documented, but a
    88	   user cannot open those panes in 2.0.0.
    89	2. **Packaged-but-not-loaded modules.** 15 files under `renderer/js/` ship inside the
    90	   package but are never loaded by the page (the R3.0E module family, the R3.0C
    91	   authority-pipeline modules superseded by the bundled contracts, the F1 migration
    92	   engine, `vehicle-profile-f312.js`, `i18n-r3-0e.js`). They are inert in the packaged
    93	   app: nothing executes them at runtime.
    94	3. **No custom app icon.** `package.json`'s build config intentionally declares no icon;
    95	   platforms show the default Electron icon.
    96	4. **No license granted.** `package.json` declares `"license": "UNLICENSED"` and the
    97	   repository carries no LICENSE file: all rights reserved; this public repository does
    98	   not currently grant reuse rights. Adopting a license is a product decision deferred
    99	   past 2.0.0.
   100	5. **No tracked lockfile (by policy).** The repository's verification lane is
   101	   dependency-free and `scripts/check-version-policy.js` enforces that
   102	   `package-lock.json` stays untracked. A fresh `npm install` resolves
   103	   `electron ^33.0.0` / `electron-builder ^25.0.0` by semver range; build reproducibility
   104	   is bounded by those ranges, not a pinned lock. This is an accepted, documented
   105	   trade-off of the dependency-free governance lane.
   106	6. **Vendored renderer libraries.** Three third-party libraries ship vendored under
   107	   `renderer/lib/`, outside npm governance: `alpine.min.js` (Alpine.js), `chart.min.js`
   108	   (Chart.js 4.4.7), `tailwind.js` (Tailwind CSS browser build). They load from disk
   109	   only (no CDN at runtime); no integrity hashes are recorded in-repo.
   110	7. **Electron boot smoke is structural.** `tests/e2e/flow-09` validates declaration-level
   111	   Electron structure (main/preload/webPreferences) without launching a window; no test
   112	   boots the real Electron binary in CI (CI is install-free by policy).
   113	8. **IndexedDB-specific failure paths untested in Node.** Quota-exceeded mapping and
   114	   `tx.onabort` in `renderer/js/storage-backend.js` have no automated coverage (Node
   115	   tests run on MemoryBackend); storage-failure semantics are covered at the transact
   116	   contract level (`hardening-02`).
   117	9. **No professional validation.** The product is not a professional race-engineer
   118	   replacement; outputs are advisory, in physical units, with explicit credibility and
   119	   limitations metadata (see `docs/r3-credibility-model.md`).
   120	10. **Preload `electronAPI` surface is not functional (pre-existing since v1.0.0).**
   121	   `preload.js` calls `require('./package.json')`, which Electron's default-sandboxed
   122	   preload cannot resolve — the preload script fails to load and `window.electronAPI`
   123	   (`{platform, version}`) is never exposed in the packaged app. The renderer's
   124	   `typeof`-guard fallback masks this (the in-app version string falls back to
   125	   `'1.0.0'`); no other functionality depends on the preload surface. Confirmed by the
   126	   F6 packaged smoke; this is NOT an F6/2.0.0 regression (present since the initial
   127	   commit) and the fix is deferred as a production change outside the 2.0.0 release
   128	   scope. `docs/r3-data-and-privacy.md` describes the *intended* preload surface.
   129	
   130	## Release boundary (current state at the F6 release-candidate stage)
   131	
   132	- DONE: Train PR #16 merged to `main` (merge commit `0711e74a`); the single authorized
   133	  version bump 1.4.0 → 2.0.0 is staged on the F6 release branch.
   134	- NOT DONE (each requires separate explicit authorization): `v2.0.0` git tag, GitHub
   135	  Release, DMG upload/binary distribution, deployment.
   136	- No code signing, no notarization (no signing identity or notary credentials exist;
   137	  see "Installation status" above).
   138	- Parallel known follow-up, NOT part of 2.0.0: the R2.4-era credibility-rung string
   139	  cleanup (three pre-Train files carrying `'Measured (kinematic, confounded)'` as a
   140	  rung value) is in progress in a separate workstream — see "Known limitations" item 5;
   141	  it must not be assumed merged.
   142	- License note: `package.json` declares `UNLICENSED` (no LICENSE file exists);
   143	  a legacy `license: MIT` field in `CITATION.cff` contradicted this and was removed
   144	  at the F6 stage — adopting an actual license remains an explicit product decision.
     1	# Changelog
     2	
     3	All notable changes to the Racing Setup Analyzer are documented in this file.
     4	
     5	The format follows a conventional changelog layout (Keep-a-Changelog style) tailored to the
     6	R3.0 Integrated Delivery Train. Each entry preserves the honesty contract: every shipped
     7	capability is described with credibility, provenance, fail-closed boundaries, and limitations.
     8	The credibility ladder used throughout is:
     9	
    10	**Physics > Model > Measured > Derived > Heuristic > Unavailable**
    11	
    12	No conclusion documented below claims a credibility rung higher than the evidence supports.
    13	When any required input or quality gate is missing, the capability is **blocked with a reason**,
    14	never approximated.
    15	
    16	---
    17	
    18	## [2.0.0] — Release Candidate (publication pending explicit authorization)
    19	
    20	The R3.0 Integrated Delivery Train (A through F) ships as a single release. The Train
    21	PR (#16) is **merged to `main`** (merge commit `0711e74a`) and the single authorized
    22	`F6_RELEASE` version bump 1.4.0 → 2.0.0 is staged on the F6 release branch (the F5
    23	release gate PASSED 12/12 before the merge; `package.json` had remained pinned at
    24	`1.4.0` until this bump). The `v2.0.0` tag and the GitHub Release have **not** been
    25	created — each requires its own explicit authorization; nothing in this section implies
    26	the release has been published.
    27	
    28	### Frozen invariants carried into the 2.0.0 candidate
    29	
    30	- The R3 case-record schema introduced in R3.0B is **frozen** and was not modified by any
    31	  later milestone (R3.0C through R3.0F).
    32	- Preset count = **501**, byte-for-byte unchanged through the train.
    33	- `featureRegistryActivationAllowed` is tracked **per phase**, not as one train-wide
    34	  switch. R3.0C, R3.0D, and R3.0E have each already flipped their own flag to `true`
    35	  (the Comparisons pane is live and usable today; the Engineer Brief, Experiment Loop,
    36	  and Case Timeline nav nodes are live in navigation, but their pane content is not yet
    37	  wired into `renderer/index.html` — the D5 mount ships hidden/inert and the page loads
    38	  no R3.0E viewmodel script); only R3.0F's own flag remains **false** until `F6_RELEASE`,
    39	  and R3.0F introduces no case-scoped pane of its own. `runtimeConsumersAllowed`
    40	  has been `true` since F1.
    41	- `canonicalTrustUpgraded` remains a hard literal `false`. Suspension normalization is numeric
    42	  compatibility, not evidence.
    43	- Electron host `webPreferences` explicitly sets exactly three keys — `preload`,
    44	  `contextIsolation: true`, `nodeIntegration: false`; no safety-relevant key (`sandbox`,
    45	  `webSecurity`, etc.) is ever explicitly weakened, so Electron's own defaults apply to
    46	  everything else. The preload bridge exposes exactly `{ platform, version }` on
    47	  `window.electronAPI` — nothing else.
    48	- **Comparison is same-case + same-session only.** Cross-case comparison and cross-session
    49	  comparison within a single case are both **permanently forbidden** at every layer
    50	  (authority, view model, export, decision engine, experiment loop).
    51	
    52	### Honesty boundaries reaffirmed
    53	
    54	- The product is not a professional race-engineer replacement.
    55	- The product is not a complete multi-body-dynamics simulator and not a complete tyre model.
    56	- No runtime LLM holds decision authority. No causation claim is made from correlation.
    57	- No driver blame is emitted by any service.
    58	- No automatic setup application. The Engineer Brief itself is built exclusively from
    59	  closure-private-WeakSet-verified R3.0D inputs (authoritative-only D2-D5 chain); the
    60	  downstream R3.0E experiment record retains it only as a grammar-checked id reference,
    61	  not a re-verified authority token — see "Engineer Brief" below.
    62	
    63	---
    64	
    65	## R3.0F — Integrated delivery (summary)
    66	
    67	R3.0F integrates the prior milestones (A through E) into a shippable workspace, with a
    68	deterministic migration path from the 1.4.0 baseline, end-to-end flow coverage, and a
    69	hardening sweep. R3.0F adds no new capability ladders of its own; it ratifies and guards the
    70	ladders that A through E established.
    71	
    72	### F1 — Migration engine
    73	
    74	- **Deterministic migration from the 1.4.0 baseline toward the 2.0.0 release candidate; the
    75	  version remains pinned at 1.4.0 until `F6_RELEASE`.** Same inputs + same
    76	  injected clock + same backend state → identical writes and identical journal entries.
    77	- **Idempotent.** Running `migrate({confirm:true})` against a fully-migrated store performs
    78	  zero writes and zero journal appends.
    79	- **Fail-closed**: any unrecognized record shape, any schema drift outside the R3.0B-frozen
    80	  surface, or any future-version record → migration blocked with a reason code; no silent
    81	  best-effort upgrade. Future-version records are **rejected** with
    82	  `UNSUPPORTED_FUTURE_VERSION` — never coerced.
    83	- **Structured-clone-only firewall** at the persistence boundary. `structuredClone` is
    84	  required at module load; the engine refuses to construct without it. Inputs that cannot be
    85	  structured-cloned are rejected at the door with `PROXY_INPUT_REJECTED`; nothing crosses
    86	  the trust boundary as a live reference.
    87	- **Trap-free JSON serializer.** `_safeJsonStringify` walks own enumerable keys directly and
    88	  **never invokes any `toJSON` hook**, defending against a hostile `Object.prototype.toJSON`
    89	  installed after engine load. Getters, proxies, and throwing accessors cannot be used to
    90	  exfiltrate state or poison output during serialization.
     1	# Racing Setup Analyzer
     2	
     3	**A case-centric vehicle-dynamics workspace that predicts handling balance, sizes your spring / ARB / damper numbers, simulates a tyre stint, and — when you bring real telemetry and a verified calibration — reports honestly-gated measured metrics. Every number is labelled by how it was derived; every blocked feature says why.**
     4	
     5	`🌐 Language:` **English** ｜ [繁體中文](#繁體中文) ｜ [日本語](#日本語)
     6	
     7	Grounded in textbook vehicle dynamics (Milliken, Gillespie, OptimumG) and cross-checked against real-world engineering practice. Runs as an Electron desktop app or straight in a browser — no build step, no server, no account; the physics runs locally in plain JavaScript.
     8	
     9	## Core value
    10	
    11	- **Transparent balance prediction** — an understeer-gradient model that combines weight distribution, roll-stiffness distribution (LLTD) and tyre load sensitivity, with every output badged **Physics / Model / Heuristic**.
    12	- **Honest, fail-closed telemetry analysis** — import a CSV, map channels, confirm identity; a *measured* understeer gradient (K_us) is produced **only** when a verified steering calibration *and* the data quality pass every gate — otherwise it stays blocked with a reason.
    13	- **Local-first case workspace** — build an Analysis Case (vehicle + setup + optional telemetry), save / reopen / duplicate / archive / export — all in a local Case Library. No cloud, no accounts; raw telemetry never leaves your device.
    14	- **Trilingual UI** — English / 繁體中文（台灣）/ 日本語, switchable at runtime across the app shell and the full analysis workspace.
    15	- **Source as teaching material** — embedded "knowledge essays" (`docs/fsae/`) walk intuition → physics → formula → code for FSAE / track-day engineers.
    16	
    17	## Current status
    18	
    19	| Milestone | Scope | State |
    20	|---|---|---|
    21	| R1 / R2.0–R2.2 | Handling prediction, advisor, spring/ARB, tyre analysis, Lihpao lap+stint, 501-car presets, Analysis Workspace slice | ✅ complete |
    22	| R2.3 | Real/imported CSV → canonical telemetry session, channel mapping + confirmation, closed-schema case export | ✅ complete |
    23	| R2.4 | Honestly-gated **measured** understeer gradient K_us (needs a verified steering calibration) + model-vs-actual | ✅ complete |
    24	| R2.5 | Setup A/B (predicted balance deltas) + quantitative recommendation in physical units | ✅ complete |
    25	| R2.6 | Track intelligence + per-corner driver steering-behaviour coaching (Heuristic, low-confidence) | ✅ complete |
    26	| R3.0A | Case-centric app shell (nav / Case Context / Trust panel) + documentation consolidation | ✅ complete |
    27	| R3.0B | Local-first Case Library & persistence (save / reopen / duplicate / archive / export-import) | ✅ complete |
    28	| R3.0C | Reference lap & corner-delta intelligence (same-case, same-session, cross-lap comparison; explicit user-selected reference only) | ✅ complete — Comparisons pane live |
    29	| R3.0D | Race-engineer decision engine (evidence graph → hypothesis → priority → advisory Engineer Brief; no runtime LLM, no causation, no driver blame) | ✅ complete — services shipped; Brief pane not yet wired into the page (nav node live, mount inert) |
    30	| R3.0E | Experiment-outcome loop (experiment / outcome / follow-up link / append-only timeline stores + contracts) | ✅ complete — services shipped; Experiment Loop / Case Timeline panes not yet wired into the page (nav nodes live) |
    31	| R3.0F | Migration engine, E2E flows, hardening probes, documentation, release gate | ✅ complete; Train merged to `main`; v2.0.0 release candidate staged (tag/Release pending authorization) |
    32	
    33	Version 2.0.0 (release candidate — the v2.0.0 tag and GitHub Release are pending explicit authorization). The Comparisons pane is live and usable; the Engineer Brief / Experiment Loop / Case Timeline panes are the documented remaining wire-in work (see `docs/release-notes-2.0.0.md`, "Known limitations").
    34	
    35	## What it can do
    36	
    37	**Works on model inputs alone**
    38	- Handling-balance prediction (understeer gradient, LLTD, roll gradient, ride frequency) — *Physics / Model*
    39	- Setup Advisor with concrete target values — *Heuristic*
    40	- Spring / ARB calculator (ride-freq ↔ rate, ARB sizing) — *Physics / Derived*
    41	- Tyre analysis + 21-tyre track-day database — *Model / Derived*
    42	- Lihpao G2 lap-time + tyre-stint simulation — *Model*
    43	- 501-car preset database (each parameter graded `confirmed` / `documented` / `estimated` / `unknown`)
    44	- Analysis Case model prediction + directional tendency — *Model / Heuristic*
    45	
    46	**Conditionally available (need real telemetry and/or calibration; gated)**
    47	- Telemetry observation (directional tendency from data) — needs **confirmed** channels
    48	- **Measured understeer gradient K_us** — needs confirmed telemetry **+ a verified road-wheel steering calibration**
    49	- Model-vs-actual comparison — directional always; magnitude only when measured K_us passes its gates
    50	- Setup A/B (predicted balance deltas — never a lap-time claim)
    51	- Quantitative setup recommendation in physical units (Nm/deg, N/mm, %)
    52	- Corner coaching (the **driver's** raw-steering behaviour per entry/mid/exit — never a vehicle or setup finding)
    53	
    54	See [`docs/r2-capability-map.md`](docs/r2-capability-map.md) for the full Available / Conditionally-Available / Blocked / Deferred matrix with each capability's required input, credibility, fail-closed conditions and limitations.
    55	
    56	## What it does not claim
    57	
    58	This tool is deliberately honest about its limits. It is **not**:
    59	
    60	- a professional race-engineer replacement, and it is never presented as professionally validated;
    61	- a full multi-body-dynamics (MBD) simulator — the model is a transparent linear/quasi-static balance model;
    62	- a complete tyre model — coefficients are estimated unless you import your own `.tir` (pure-slip lateral only);
    63	- a GPS racing-line / lap-optimization tool — no racing line, no exact apex from position alone;
    64	- a telemetry decoder for arbitrary binary formats — the production path is CSV / canonical-JSON;
    65	- a source of hardware "clicks" — there is no validated per-car click→rate mapping, so output stays in physical units;
    66	- a MoTeC / commercial-analysis replacement.
    67	
    68	Measured numbers are never claimed without the data **and** calibration to back them, and nothing is fabricated to fill a gap.
    69	
    70	## Credibility model
    71	
    72	Every value carries a badge for **how it was derived**, and every conclusion carries trust metadata (credibility · confidence · provenance · limitations · blockers · evidence references · next validation step). This is enforced in the services, not the UI.
    73	
    74	- **◆ Physics** — closed-form physical relation (exact for the inputs given). *≠ Model.*
    75	- **◈ Model** — a model prediction built from physics relations. *≠ Measured.*
    76	- **Measured** — derived from real telemetry; still confounded (tyre/track/driver/sensor), with machine-read provenance (synthetic / real / unverified). *≠ fully validated.*
    77	- **Derived** — a deterministic transform of inputs. *≠ direct measurement.*
    78	- **◇ Heuristic** — a rule-of-thumb observation. *≠ a vehicle fact.*
    79	- **Unavailable** — not derivable from the present evidence (shown blocked, with a reason).
    80	
    81	**Fail-closed, always:** when a required input or quality gate is missing, the capability is blocked with a reason, never approximated. Driver behaviour ≠ vehicle characteristic ≠ setup finding; correlation ≠ causation; prediction ≠ guaranteed result. See [`docs/credibility-and-trust.md`](docs/credibility-and-trust.md).
    82	
    83	## Main workflow
    84	
    85	1. **Create an Analysis Case** — pick a vehicle + setup (or load the synthetic Demo Case).
    86	2. **(Optional) Import telemetry** — CSV / canonical-JSON → map raw columns to canonical channels → confirm identity.
    87	3. **Run the analysis** — model prediction + (if telemetry is confirmed) a directional observation.
    88	4. **(Optional) Add a steering calibration** — unlocks the honestly-gated measured K_us and magnitude comparison.
    89	5. **Review** — model-vs-actual, measured metrics, Setup A/B, quantitative recommendation, corner coaching — each with its credibility and blockers.
    90	6. **Save to the Case Library** — reopen-identical, duplicate, archive, or export a portable (curated, raw-free) bundle.
    91	
    92	## UI overview
    93	
    94	A case-centric shell: a left **Workspace** nav (Dashboard / Analysis Cases / Import Telemetry / Setup Library / Comparisons / Settings), a per-case nav (Overview / Setup & Model / Telemetry / Measured Metrics / Model vs Actual / Recommendations / Corner Coaching / Evidence & Trust), a **Case Context** bar, and a fixed **Context/Trust** panel that re-reads the services' capability and surfaces status, credibility, blockers and the next action. The Setup Library hosts the original calculators (Spring / Tyre / Advisor / Lihpao Lap / raw-CSV Telemetry Viewer).
    95	
    96	## Installation / run
    97	
    98	```bash
    99	# Desktop (Electron)
   100	npm install
   101	npm start
   102	
   103	# Or open it in a browser (no server needed — pure client-side JS)
   104	open renderer/index.html        # macOS
   105	
   106	# Run the full test suite (physics regression + telemetry + persistence + UI contract + i18n parity)
   107	npm test
   108	```
   109	
   110	## Data and privacy
   111	
   112	- **Local-first.** Cases and sessions are stored in the browser/Electron IndexedDB on your machine. No cloud, no accounts, no sign-in.
   113	- **No tracking.** No analytics, no third-party telemetry, no tracking pixels, no cookies.
   114	- **Raw telemetry stays on device.** It lives in a capacity-bounded local session store and is **never** included in a portable export. A portable case bundle is a curated, value-constrained, raw-free summary; an imported bundle opens as an explicitly degraded "imported summary".
   115	- **Imported `.tir` / CSV files are read locally** and never uploaded or bundled.
   116

   128	## Delivery status (R3.0 Train)
   129	
   130	- **R3.0C** — reference lap & corner-delta intelligence: a normalized track-position axis, per-corner + entry/mid/exit deltas, with a hard comparability gate. *Shipped; the Comparisons pane is live in the app.*
   131	- **R3.0D** — a deterministic, evidence-backed virtual race-engineer decision engine (primary issue + alternatives + a verifiable next experiment; never a free-text LLM conclusion). *Services shipped; the Brief pane is not yet wired into the page (nav node live, mount inert).*
   132	- **R3.0E** — a recommendation experiment & outcome loop (expected vs observed; local evidence history only). *Services and stores shipped; the Experiment Loop / Case Timeline panes are not yet wired into the page (nav nodes live).*
   133	- **R3.0F** — product hardening, schema migration, documentation and the release gate. *Complete; the Train is merged to `main` and the 2.0.0 version bump is staged; the v2.0.0 tag and GitHub Release remain pending explicit authorization.*
   134	
   135	Anything not yet wired is never presented in the app as if it already works — unwired panes stay behind inert mounts or nav-only nodes (see `docs/release-notes-2.0.0.md`, "Known limitations").
   136	
   137	## Validation and limitations
   138	
   139	- The model is validated against textbook formulas and real-world engineering practice (e.g. critical damping `Cc = 2√(k·m)`, axle roll stiffness `k·t²/2`); see [`docs/physics-notes.md`](docs/physics-notes.md). The automated tests are **synthetic** regression/contract tests — they are not real-world track validation.
   140	- **Absolute lap times depend entirely on your inputs.** Published circuit records are usually set by heavily-modified cars, so a stock-spec prediction being slower is correct, not a bug. The tool's strength is **relative** comparison and tyre-stint sweet-spot analysis; methodology transfers across cars, absolute numbers do not — especially for FSAE, where you must fit your own tyre data.
   141	- **Known boundaries:** no suspension hardpoint kinematics beyond the 2D front-view double-wishbone calculator; no transient model beyond a linear 2-DOF step-steer; one representative track for lap simulation; tyre coefficients estimated unless a `.tir` is imported.
   142	
   143	> **⚠️ Legacy / historical research path.** The `.bmsbin` clean-room binary investigation (`docs/phase-3-trust-chain.md` + the `bmsbin-*` notes) is a **historical research path** that decodes nothing beyond a channel catalog and produces no canonical telemetry. The **production telemetry path is CSV / canonical-JSON import** (R2.3+) — the only route that yields a Canonical Telemetry Session, directional analysis and (with a verified calibration) a measured K_us. The two are intentionally separate.
   144

   146	
   147	MIT-spirit personal/educational project — contributions and corrections welcome. Built in Taiwan, aimed at making serious chassis-engineering knowledge accessible to the next generation of FSAE students and track-day engineers.

   152	## 繁體中文
   153	
   154	# Racing Setup Analyzer — 賽車調校與單圈分析工具
   155	
   156	**以「分析案例」為核心的車輛動力學工作台：預測操控平衡、算出彈簧／防傾桿／阻尼數值、模擬輪胎 stint；當你帶入真實遙測與一份經驗證的校正時，輸出誠實受閘門控管的量測指標。每個數字都標示它是怎麼推導出來的，每個被擋下的功能都說明原因。**
   157	
   158	`🌐 語言:` [English](#racing-setup-analyzer) ｜ **繁體中文** ｜ [日本語](#日本語)
   159	
   160	建立在標準車輛動力學教科書（Milliken、Gillespie、OptimumG）之上，並用真實世界的工程實務交叉驗證。可作為 Electron 桌面 app，或直接用瀏覽器開——免編譯、免伺服器、免帳號，物理全在本地 JavaScript 執行。
   161	
   162	### 核心價值
   163	
   164	- **透明的平衡預測** — 轉向不足梯度模型整合配重、側傾剛度分配（LLTD）與輪胎負載敏感度，每個輸出都標 **物理計算（Physics）／模型結果（Model）／啟發式判定（Heuristic）**。
   165	- **誠實、fail-closed 的遙測分析** — 匯入 CSV、對應通道、確認身分；**唯有**當一份經驗證的轉向校正*與*資料品質全數通過閘門，才會輸出*量測所得*的轉向不足梯度（K_us）——否則維持「無法執行」並附原因。
   166	- **本機優先的案例工作台** — 建立分析案例（車輛＋設定＋可選遙測），可儲存／重新開啟／建立副本／封存／匯出，全在本機案例庫。無雲端、無帳號；原始遙測資料絕不離開你的裝置。
   167	- **三語介面** — English／繁體中文（台灣）／日本語，可即時切換，涵蓋 app 外殼與完整分析工作台。
   168	- **原始碼即教材** — 內嵌寫給 FSAE／賽道工程師的「知識文」（`docs/fsae/`）：直覺 → 物理 → 公式 → 程式碼。
   169	
   170	### 目前狀態
   171	
   172	| 里程碑 | 範圍 | 狀態 |
   173	|---|---|---|
   174	| R1 / R2.0–R2.2 | 操控預測、調校建議、彈簧／ARB、輪胎分析、麗寶單圈+stint、501 車預設、分析工作台切片 | ✅ 完成 |
   175	| R2.3 | 真實／匯入 CSV → 標準化遙測 session、通道對應+確認、封閉 schema 案例匯出 | ✅ 完成 |
   176	| R2.4 | 誠實受閘門控管的*量測*轉向不足梯度 K_us（需經驗證的轉向校正）+ model-vs-actual | ✅ 完成 |
   177	| R2.5 | Setup A/B（預測平衡差）+ 物理單位的量化建議 | ✅ 完成 |
   178	| R2.6 | 賽道情報 + 逐彎駕駛轉向行為教練（啟發式、低信心） | ✅ 完成 |
   179	| R3.0A | 案例為核心的 app 外殼（導覽／Case Context／可信度面板）+ 文件整併 | ✅ 完成 |
   180	| R3.0B | 本機優先案例庫與持久化（儲存／重開／副本／封存／匯出匯入） | ✅ 完成 |

./docs/r3-user-workflow.md:11:A note on version numbers used in this document: "the case-record schema frozen at v1.4.0" is a human-readable label for "the on-disk shape of a Case record as it stood when the package version was 1.4.0" — it is **not** a literal version string stored in code. The actual schema-version field is `CASE_SCHEMA_VERSION` in `renderer/js/schema-migration.js`, a plain integer (`1` today), and F1's migrators compare against that integer, not against any `"v1.4.0"` string. The **package application version** is `1.4.0` today, with a target of `2.0.0` post-merge of the integrated delivery train. The two are related but distinct: a future application version bump does not, on its own, advance `CASE_SCHEMA_VERSION`.
./docs/r3-user-workflow.md:37:A Case is the case-centric primary object defined in R3.0A and persisted under the R3 case-record schema frozen at `v1.4.0` in R3.0B. The schema has not been modified through R3.0F and will not be modified until a future major; F1 migration treats `v1.4.0` as the baseline.

./governance/r3.0f/codex-checkpoint-evidence/F4/round-27-4f4338a-PASS.raw.txt:892:    11	A note on version numbers used in this document: "the case-record schema frozen at v1.4.0" is a human-readable label for "the on-disk shape of a Case record as it stood when the package version was 1.4.0" — it is **not** a literal version string stored in code. The actual schema-version field is `CASE_SCHEMA_VERSION` in `renderer/js/schema-migration.js`, a plain integer (`1` today), and F1's migrators compare against that integer, not against any `"v1.4.0"` string. The **package application version** is `1.4.0` today, with a target of `2.0.0` post-merge of the integrated delivery train. The two are related but distinct: a future application version bump does not, on its own, advance `CASE_SCHEMA_VERSION`.

./governance/r3.0f/codex-checkpoint-evidence/F4/round-27-4f4338a-PASS.raw.txt:7507:11:A note on version numbers used in this document: "the case-record schema frozen at v1.4.0" is a human-readable label for "the on-disk shape of a Case record as it stood when the package version was 1.4.0" — it is **not** a literal version string stored in code. The actual schema-version field is `CASE_SCHEMA_VERSION` in `renderer/js/schema-migration.js`, a plain integer (`1` today), and F1's migrators compare against that integer, not against any `"v1.4.0"` string. The **package application version** is `1.4.0` today, with a target of `2.0.0` post-merge of the integrated delivery train. The two are related but distinct: a future application version bump does not, on its own, advance `CASE_SCHEMA_VERSION`.

     5	 * NAV_NODES — the navigation STRUCTURE (shell sections / setup-library areas / case subviews) that is NOT itself a
     6	 *             feature but is needed to render navigation (Dashboard/Import/Settings/Comparisons + overview/telemetry).
     7	 *
     8	 * Every navigation surface (mainNav, caseNav, the former caseSubviewIds, setupLibraryTabs, the showPane /
     9	 * setShellSection / setCaseSubview routing) is a PROJECTION of this one structure via the derivation functions
    10	 * below — no second whitelist. The only place legacy UI-state names (shellSection/currentTab/caseSubview/paneId)
    11	 * live is `NAV_NODES[].legacyRouting` + `FEATURES[].rendererAdapter`.
    12	 *
    13	 * UMD: Node require / Electron renderer global (FeatureRegistry).
    14	 */
    15	(function (root) {
    16	  'use strict';
    17	
    18	  // ── NAV_NODES — navigation structure (not features) ──
    19	  var NAV_NODES = {
    20	    dashboard: { id: 'dashboard', kind: 'shell_section', labelKey: 'nav.dashboard', order: 0, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'dashboard', currentTab: 'dashboard' } },
    21	    cases: { id: 'cases', kind: 'shell_section', labelKey: 'nav.cases', order: 1, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'cases', caseSubview: 'overview', currentTab: 'analysis' } },
    22	    import: { id: 'import', kind: 'shell_section', labelKey: 'nav.import', order: 2, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'import', caseSubview: 'overview', currentTab: 'analysis' } },
    23	    setup_library: { id: 'setup_library', kind: 'shell_section', labelKey: 'nav.setup_library', order: 3, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'setup_library' } },
    24	    comparisons: { id: 'comparisons', kind: 'shell_section', labelKey: 'nav.comparisons', order: 4, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'comparisons', currentTab: 'comparisons' } },
    25	    settings: { id: 'settings', kind: 'shell_section', labelKey: 'nav.settings', order: 5, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'settings', currentTab: 'guide' } },
    26	    // setup-library areas
    27	    'setuplib:vehicle_setup': { id: 'setuplib:vehicle_setup', kind: 'setuplib_area', parentNodeId: 'setup_library', labelKey: 'setuplib.area.vehicle_setup', order: 0, availability: 'available' },
    28	    'setuplib:engineering_tools': { id: 'setuplib:engineering_tools', kind: 'setuplib_area', parentNodeId: 'setup_library', labelKey: 'setuplib.area.engineering_tools', order: 1, availability: 'available' },
    29	    'setuplib:analysis_support': { id: 'setuplib:analysis_support', kind: 'setuplib_area', parentNodeId: 'setup_library', labelKey: 'setuplib.area.analysis_support', order: 2, availability: 'available' },
    30	    // case subviews (parent 'cases'); overview + telemetry are nav nodes, not features
    31	    'case:overview': { id: 'case:overview', kind: 'case_subview', parentNodeId: 'cases', subviewId: 'overview', labelKey: 'casenav.overview', order: 0, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'cases', caseSubview: 'overview', currentTab: 'analysis' } },
    32	    'case:setup_model': { id: 'case:setup_model', kind: 'case_subview', parentNodeId: 'cases', subviewId: 'setup_model', labelKey: 'casenav.setup_model', order: 1, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'cases', caseSubview: 'setup_model', currentTab: 'predict' } },
    33	    'case:telemetry': { id: 'case:telemetry', kind: 'case_subview', parentNodeId: 'cases', subviewId: 'telemetry', labelKey: 'casenav.telemetry', order: 2, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'cases', caseSubview: 'telemetry', currentTab: 'analysis' } },
    34	    'case:measured_metrics': { id: 'case:measured_metrics', kind: 'case_subview', parentNodeId: 'cases', subviewId: 'measured_metrics', labelKey: 'casenav.measured_metrics', order: 3, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'cases', caseSubview: 'measured_metrics', currentTab: 'analysis' } },
    35	    'case:model_vs_actual': { id: 'case:model_vs_actual', kind: 'case_subview', parentNodeId: 'cases', subviewId: 'model_vs_actual', labelKey: 'casenav.model_vs_actual', order: 4, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'cases', caseSubview: 'model_vs_actual', currentTab: 'analysis' } },
    36	    'case:recommendations': { id: 'case:recommendations', kind: 'case_subview', parentNodeId: 'cases', subviewId: 'recommendations', labelKey: 'casenav.recommendations', order: 5, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'cases', caseSubview: 'recommendations', currentTab: 'analysis' } },
    37	    'case:corner_coaching': { id: 'case:corner_coaching', kind: 'case_subview', parentNodeId: 'cases', subviewId: 'corner_coaching', labelKey: 'casenav.corner_coaching', order: 6, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'cases', caseSubview: 'corner_coaching', currentTab: 'analysis' } },
    38	    'case:evidence_trust': { id: 'case:evidence_trust', kind: 'case_subview', parentNodeId: 'cases', subviewId: 'evidence_trust', labelKey: 'casenav.evidence_trust', order: 7, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'cases', caseSubview: 'evidence_trust', currentTab: 'analysis' } },
    39	    // R3.0D D5 — Engineer Brief subview. NAV NODE ONLY at this milestone: the D5 mount in
    40	    // renderer/index.html ships hidden/inert and no UI event calls the orchestrator's
    41	    // prepareEngineerInsight — pane content is a downstream wire-in step (disclosed in
    42	    // docs/release-notes-2.0.0.md "Known limitations"). Activation governed by
    43	    // governance/r3.0d/state.json (featureRegistryActivationAllowed=true at
    44	    // D5_ENGINEER_BRIEF_ACTIVATION).
    45	    'case:engineer_brief': { id: 'case:engineer_brief', kind: 'case_subview', parentNodeId: 'cases', subviewId: 'engineer_brief', labelKey: 'casenav.engineer_brief', order: 8, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'cases', caseSubview: 'engineer_brief', currentTab: 'analysis' } },
    46	    // R3.0E E5 — Experiment Loop + Case Timeline subviews. NAV NODES ONLY at this milestone:
    47	    // renderer/index.html loads no R3.0E viewmodel script and renders no pane content for
    48	    // either subview — pane wiring is a downstream step (disclosed in
    49	    // docs/release-notes-2.0.0.md "Known limitations"). Activation governed by
    50	    // governance/r3.0e/state.json (featureRegistryActivationAllowed=true at E5_UI_ACTIVATION).
    51	    'case:experiment_loop': { id: 'case:experiment_loop', kind: 'case_subview', parentNodeId: 'cases', subviewId: 'experiment_loop', labelKey: 'casenav.experiment_loop', order: 9, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'cases', caseSubview: 'experiment_loop', currentTab: 'analysis' } },
    52	    'case:case_timeline': { id: 'case:case_timeline', kind: 'case_subview', parentNodeId: 'cases', subviewId: 'case_timeline', labelKey: 'casenav.case_timeline', order: 10, availability: 'available', legacyRouting: { shellSection: 'cases', caseSubview: 'case_timeline', currentTab: 'analysis' } },
    53	  };
    54	
    55	  // ── FEATURES — the 23 stable Feature IDs ──
    56	  function F(id, area, navNodeId, paneId, opts) {
    57	    opts = opts || {};
    58	    var e = { id: id, area: area, labelKey: 'feat.' + id, descriptionKey: 'feat.' + id + '.desc', availability: opts.availability || 'available', navNodeId: navNodeId, capabilities: opts.capabilities || [], allowedActions: opts.allowedActions || [], entryPoints: opts.entryPoints || { desktop: true, mobile: true } };
    59	    if (opts.deferredReason) e.deferredReason = opts.deferredReason;
    60	    if (paneId) e.rendererAdapter = { paneId: paneId, focusTarget: opts.focusTarget || null };
    61	    return e;
    62	  }
    63	  var FEATURES = {
    64	    // A — Vehicle & Setup (predict pane → fixes the Setup-Library orphan)
    65	    vehicle_presets: F('vehicle_presets', 'vehicle_setup', 'setuplib:vehicle_setup', 'predict', { focusTarget: 'vp-browser', allowedActions: ['browse', 'build_draft'] }),
    66	    vehicle_preset_detail: F('vehicle_preset_detail', 'vehicle_setup', 'setuplib:vehicle_setup', 'predict', { focusTarget: 'vp-detail', allowedActions: ['browse'], entryPoints: { desktop: false, mobile: false } }),
    67	    custom_setup: F('custom_setup', 'vehicle_setup', 'setuplib:vehicle_setup', 'predict', { focusTarget: 'custom-setup', allowedActions: ['edit'] }),
    68	    handling_prediction: F('handling_prediction', 'vehicle_setup', 'setuplib:vehicle_setup', 'predict', { focusTarget: 'handling-gauge', capabilities: ['Physics', 'Model'] }),
    69	    // B — Engineering Tools
    70	    spring_calculator: F('spring_calculator', 'engineering_tools', 'setuplib:engineering_tools', 'spring'),
    71	    arb_calculator: F('arb_calculator', 'engineering_tools', 'setuplib:engineering_tools', 'spring', { focusTarget: 'arb-section' }),
    72	    suspension_kinematics: F('suspension_kinematics', 'engineering_tools', 'setuplib:engineering_tools', 'spring', { focusTarget: 'kinematics-section' }),
    73	    corner_weight: F('corner_weight', 'engineering_tools', 'setuplib:engineering_tools', 'predict', { focusTarget: 'corner-weight-section' }),
    74	    tire_analysis: F('tire_analysis', 'engineering_tools', 'setuplib:engineering_tools', 'tire'),
    75	    wheel_upgrade: F('wheel_upgrade', 'engineering_tools', 'setuplib:engineering_tools', 'predict', { focusTarget: 'wheel-upgrade-section' }),
    76	    // C — Analysis & Support
    77	    setup_advisor: F('setup_advisor', 'analysis_support', 'setuplib:analysis_support', 'advisor'),
    78	    lihpao_simulator: F('lihpao_simulator', 'analysis_support', 'setuplib:analysis_support', 'lihpao'),
    79	    telemetry_viewer: F('telemetry_viewer', 'analysis_support', 'setuplib:analysis_support', 'telemetry'),
    80	    // Case-scoped
    81	    analysis_cases: F('analysis_cases', 'case_scoped', 'cases', 'analysis'),
    82	    case_setup_model: F('case_setup_model', 'case_scoped', 'case:setup_model', 'predict'),
    83	    measured_metrics: F('measured_metrics', 'case_scoped', 'case:measured_metrics', 'analysis', { availability: 'available_conditional' }),
    84	    model_vs_actual: F('model_vs_actual', 'case_scoped', 'case:model_vs_actual', 'analysis', { availability: 'available_conditional' }),
    85	    recommendations: F('recommendations', 'case_scoped', 'case:recommendations', 'analysis', { availability: 'available_conditional' }),
    86	    corner_coaching: F('corner_coaching', 'case_scoped', 'case:corner_coaching', 'analysis', { availability: 'available_conditional' }),
    87	    evidence_trust: F('evidence_trust', 'case_scoped', 'case:evidence_trust', 'analysis'),
    88	    // R3.0D D5 — Engineer Brief feature. case_scoped; available_conditional describes the
    89	    // per-case DATA gate the D5 viewmodel enforces (no authoritative D3/D4/D5 chain →
    90	    // 'unavailable' / 'inconclusive' display state). NOTE: at this milestone no shipped
    91	    // pane renders it — the index.html mount is hidden/inert (nav node only; see
    92	    // docs/release-notes-2.0.0.md "Known limitations").
    93	    engineer_brief: F('engineer_brief', 'case_scoped', 'case:engineer_brief', 'analysis', { availability: 'available_conditional' }),
    94	    // R3.0E E5 — Experiment Loop + Case Timeline features. case_scoped;
    95	    // available_conditional describes the per-case DATA gate the R3.0E experiment
    96	    // viewmodel enforces (no authoritative E3 outcome / E4 projection → 'unavailable'
    97	    // display state — exercised by the Node suites). NOTE: at this milestone
    98	    // renderer/index.html loads no R3.0E viewmodel script and renders no pane for either
    99	    // subview (nav nodes only; see docs/release-notes-2.0.0.md "Known limitations").
   100	    experiment_loop: F('experiment_loop', 'case_scoped', 'case:experiment_loop', 'analysis', { availability: 'available_conditional' }),
   101	    case_timeline: F('case_timeline', 'case_scoped', 'case:case_timeline', 'analysis', { availability: 'available_conditional' }),
   102	    // R3.0C — activated at C8_ACTIVATION (governance/r3.0c/state.json featureRegistryActivationAllowed=true).
   103	    // Pane id 'comparisons' is served by the Comparison Workspace UI (renderer/index.html data-r3c-c7-pane).
   104	    // Same-Analysis-Case / same-session scope is enforced by the orchestrator + viewmodel; feature-router only
   105	    // resolves the route (shellSection=comparisons / currentTab=comparisons via NAV_NODES.comparisons.legacyRouting).
   106	    // Mobile entry point stays false: the workspace is a multi-pane desktop surface.
   107	    case_comparison: F('case_comparison', 'case_comparison', 'comparisons', 'comparisons', { allowedActions: ['compare'], entryPoints: { desktop: true, mobile: false } }),
   108	    reference_lap: F('reference_lap', 'case_comparison', 'comparisons', 'comparisons', { allowedActions: ['select_reference'], entryPoints: { desktop: true, mobile: false } }),
   109	    corner_delta: F('corner_delta', 'case_comparison', 'comparisons', 'comparisons', { allowedActions: ['inspect_corner'], entryPoints: { desktop: true, mobile: false } }),
   110	  };
   111	
   112	  // ── derivation (the single projection algorithm) ──
   113	  function _byOrder(a, b) { return (a.order || 0) - (b.order || 0); }
   114	  function _nodes(kind) { return Object.keys(NAV_NODES).map(function (k) { return NAV_NODES[k]; }).filter(function (n) { return n.kind === kind; }).sort(_byOrder); }
   115	  function _features() { return Object.keys(FEATURES).map(function (k) { return FEATURES[k]; }); }
   116	
   117	  function deriveMainNav() { return _nodes('shell_section').map(function (n) { var o = { id: n.id, labelKey: n.labelKey }; if (n.availability === 'deferred') o.deferred = n.deferredReason; return o; }); }
   118	  function deriveCaseNav() { return _nodes('case_subview').map(function (n) { return { id: n.subviewId, labelKey: n.labelKey }; }); }
   119	  function deriveCaseSubviewIds() { return _nodes('case_subview').map(function (n) { return n.subviewId; }); }
   120	  function deriveSetupLibraryAreas() {
   121	    return _nodes('setuplib_area').map(function (area) {
   122	      var feats = _features().filter(function (f) { return f.navNodeId === area.id && f.entryPoints && f.entryPoints.desktop === true; })
   123	        .map(function (f) { return { id: f.id, labelKey: f.labelKey, descriptionKey: f.descriptionKey, availability: f.availability, allowedActions: f.allowedActions }; });
   124	      return { areaId: area.id, labelKey: area.labelKey, features: feats };
   125	    });
   126	  }
   127	  // the pane-id whitelist for the setup_library section — DERIVED (replaces the inline array; INCLUDES 'predict')
   128	  function deriveSetupLibraryPaneIds() {
   129	    var set = {}; _features().forEach(function (f) { if ((f.area === 'vehicle_setup' || f.area === 'engineering_tools' || f.area === 'analysis_support') && f.rendererAdapter) set[f.rendererAdapter.paneId] = true; });
   130	    return Object.keys(set);
   131	  }
   132	
   133	  function getFeature(id) { return FEATURES[id] || null; }
   134	  function getNode(id) { return NAV_NODES[id] || null; }
   135	
   136	  // NOTE: featureId → legacy UI-state (shellSection/currentTab/caseSubview) is constructed ONLY in feature-router.js
   137	  // (the single translator). The registry stays declarative data (FEATURES/NAV_NODES) + navigation derivations.
   138	
   139	  function getFeatureBreadcrumb(featureId) {
   140	    var f = FEATURES[featureId]; if (!f) return [];
   141	    var crumbs = [], node = NAV_NODES[f.navNodeId];
   142	    if (node && node.kind === 'setuplib_area') { crumbs.push({ id: 'setup_library', labelKey: NAV_NODES.setup_library.labelKey }); crumbs.push({ id: node.id, labelKey: node.labelKey }); }
   143	    else if (node && node.parentNodeId) { var p = NAV_NODES[node.parentNodeId]; if (p) crumbs.push({ id: p.id, labelKey: p.labelKey }); crumbs.push({ id: node.id, labelKey: node.labelKey }); }
   144	    else if (node) { crumbs.push({ id: node.id, labelKey: node.labelKey }); }
   145	    crumbs.push({ id: f.id, labelKey: f.labelKey });
   146	    return crumbs;
   147	  }
   148	  function isFeatureReachable(featureId) {
   149	    var f = FEATURES[featureId]; if (!f) return false;
   150	    if (f.availability === 'deferred') return true; // reachable as an explicit deferred panel
   151	    return !!(f.navNodeId && NAV_NODES[f.navNodeId] && (f.rendererAdapter || f.area === 'deferred'));
   152	  }
   153	  function getFeatureEntryPoints(featureId) { var f = FEATURES[featureId]; return f ? f.entryPoints : null; }
   154	
   155	  // registry self-validation (used by contract tests)
   156	  function validateRegistry() {
   157	    var errors = [];
   158	    var ids = Object.keys(FEATURES);
   159	    var seen = {};
   160	    ids.forEach(function (id) {
   161	      var f = FEATURES[id];
   162	      if (f.id !== id) errors.push('id_mismatch:' + id);
   163	      if (seen[id]) errors.push('duplicate_id:' + id); seen[id] = true;
   164	      ['id', 'area', 'labelKey', 'availability', 'navNodeId', 'entryPoints'].forEach(function (k) { if (f[k] === undefined || f[k] === null) errors.push('missing_field:' + id + '.' + k); });
   165	      if (!NAV_NODES[f.navNodeId]) errors.push('dangling_navNode:' + id + '->' + f.navNodeId);
   166	      if (f.availability === 'deferred') { if (!f.deferredReason) errors.push('deferred_no_reason:' + id); }
   167	      else if (!f.rendererAdapter) errors.push('no_renderer_adapter:' + id);
   168	      if (f.availability !== 'deferred' && !isFeatureReachable(id)) errors.push('unreachable:' + id);
   169	    });
   170	    return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors: errors };
   171	  }
   172	
   173	  var api = {
   174	    FEATURES: FEATURES, NAV_NODES: NAV_NODES,
   175	    getFeature: getFeature, getNode: getNode,
   176	    deriveMainNav: deriveMainNav, deriveCaseNav: deriveCaseNav, deriveCaseSubviewIds: deriveCaseSubviewIds,
   177	    deriveSetupLibraryAreas: deriveSetupLibraryAreas, deriveSetupLibraryPaneIds: deriveSetupLibraryPaneIds,
   178	    getFeatureBreadcrumb: getFeatureBreadcrumb,
   179	    isFeatureReachable: isFeatureReachable, getFeatureEntryPoints: getFeatureEntryPoints,
   180	    validateRegistry: validateRegistry,
   181	  };
   182	  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
   183	  if (root) root.FeatureRegistry = api;
   184	})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);
