.github/workflows/ci.yml
     1	name: trusted-verification-gate
     2	
     3	# R3-GATE0 — the authoritative remote verification gate.
     4	# Local stdout / npm exit codes / `gh pr merge` output are NOT trusted; the truth is this workflow's
     5	# conclusion + the structured artifact it uploads, both bound to a concrete commit SHA.
     6	# This workflow is read-only: it never commits, pushes, tags, releases, or mutates the repo.
     7	
     8	on:
     9	  pull_request:
    10	  push:
    11	    branches: [main]
    12	  workflow_dispatch:
    13	
    14	# Least privilege: read the checkout, nothing else. No write scopes anywhere.
    15	permissions:
    16	  contents: read
    17	
    18	concurrency:
    19	  group: trusted-verification-${{ github.event.pull_request.number || github.ref }}
    20	  cancel-in-progress: false
    21	
    22	jobs:
    23	  verify:
    24	    name: trusted-verification
    25	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    26	    # Job ceiling exceeds the SUM of EVERY step timeout below (checkout 5 + setup 5 + verifiers ≈33 ≈ 43),
    27	    # so even if every step ran to its own limit the job would not be killed before evidence collection +
    28	    # upload. A single hung step is killed at the step (a failure, NOT a cancel), and collect + upload run.
    29	    timeout-minutes: 50
    30	    env:
    31	      ARTIFACT_DIR: ${{ github.workspace }}/artifacts
    32	      TEST_TIMEOUT_MS: '180000'
    33	    steps:
    34	      # Pin every third-party Action to a full commit SHA (never a moving tag).
    35	      - name: Checkout (PR HEAD commit, not the synthetic merge ref)
    36	        uses: actions/checkout@9c091bb21b7c1c1d1991bb908d89e4e9dddfe3e0 # v7.0.0
    37	        timeout-minutes: 5
    38	        with:
    39	          fetch-depth: 0
    40	          ref: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha || github.sha }}
    41	          persist-credentials: false
    42	
    43	      - name: Setup Node 22 LTS
    44	        uses: actions/setup-node@48b55a011bda9f5d6aeb4c2d9c7362e8dae4041e # v6.4.0
    45	        timeout-minutes: 5
    46	        with:
    47	          node-version: '22'
    48	
    49	      - name: Resolve base/head identity (pass GitHub context via env, never inline)
    50	        timeout-minutes: 2
    51	        env:
    52	          EVENT_NAME: ${{ github.event_name }}
    53	          PR_BASE: ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}
    54	          PR_HEAD: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}
    55	          PUSH_BEFORE: ${{ github.event.before }}
    56	          GH_SHA: ${{ github.sha }}
    57	        run: |
    58	          set -euo pipefail
    59	          git fetch --no-tags --depth=0 origin main >/dev/null 2>&1 || git fetch --no-tags origin main >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    60	          if [ "$EVENT_NAME" = "pull_request" ]; then
    61	            BASE="$PR_BASE"; HEAD="$PR_HEAD"
    62	          elif [ "$EVENT_NAME" = "push" ]; then
    63	            BASE="$PUSH_BEFORE"; HEAD="$GH_SHA"
    64	          else
    65	            BASE="$(git rev-parse origin/main 2>/dev/null || git rev-parse HEAD~1 2>/dev/null || git rev-parse HEAD)"
    66	            HEAD="$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
    67	          fi
    68	          case "$BASE" in ""|0000000000000000000000000000000000000000) BASE="$(git rev-parse origin/main 2>/dev/null || git rev-parse HEAD)";; esac
    69	          echo "BASE_SHA=$BASE" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
    70	          echo "HEAD_SHA=$HEAD" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
    71	          echo "resolved event=$EVENT_NAME BASE_SHA=$BASE HEAD_SHA=$HEAD"
    72	
    73	      - name: Verify checked-out SHA matches the event-reported SHA
    74	        timeout-minutes: 1
    75	        env:
    76	          EVENT_NAME: ${{ github.event_name }}
    77	          GH_SHA: ${{ github.sha }}
    78	        run: |
    79	          set -euo pipefail
    80	          ACTUAL="$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
    81	          echo "checked-out=$ACTUAL  expected HEAD_SHA=$HEAD_SHA  github.sha=$GH_SHA"
    82	          if [ "$EVENT_NAME" = "pull_request" ] || [ "$EVENT_NAME" = "push" ]; then
    83	            if [ "$ACTUAL" != "$HEAD_SHA" ]; then
    84	              echo "::error::checked-out SHA ($ACTUAL) != expected HEAD_SHA ($HEAD_SHA)"; exit 1
    85	            fi
    86	          fi
    87	
    88	      # Dependency-free lane: this repo has never tracked a lockfile and verification needs no third-party
    89	      # package (deps={} ; tests/scripts use only Node builtins + relative requires). The job therefore
    90	      # installs nothing; the dependency audit below proves that contract. (Electron packaging is a
    91	      # separate future milestone, not this gate.)
    92	      - name: Confirm dependency-free lane (this job installs nothing)
    93	        timeout-minutes: 1
    94	        run: |
    95	          set -euo pipefail
    96	          echo "Dependency-free verification lane: only node scripts run here."
    97	          echo "node version: $(node -v)"
    98	          echo "npm version: $(npm -v)"
    99	
   100	      - name: A0 — Dependency audit (self-test, then prove no third-party packages reachable)
   101	        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
   102	        timeout-minutes: 2
   103	        run: |
   104	          node scripts/check-verification-dependencies.js --selftest
   105	          node scripts/check-verification-dependencies.js
   106	
   107	      - name: A — Full per-file test manifest
   108	        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
   109	        timeout-minutes: 10
   110	        run: node scripts/run-tests-manifest.js
   111	
   112	      - name: B — i18n three-language parity
   113	        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
   114	        timeout-minutes: 2
   115	        run: node scripts/check-i18n.js
   116	
   117	      - name: C — Feature registry / reachability
   118	        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
   119	        timeout-minutes: 2
   120	        run: node scripts/check-feature-registry.js
   121

   123	        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
   124	        timeout-minutes: 2

   126

   129	        timeout-minutes: 2

   131	        # feature IDs from deferred to active. The frozen-boundary mechanism uses FROZEN_ALLOW for
   132	        # exactly this milestone-authorized change. While featureRegistryActivationAllowed=false
   133	        # (C0–C7) the file is treated as fully frozen; once C8 flips the flag the allowlist is
   134	        # populated for this single file only — any OTHER frozen file touched still fails the check.

   138	            ALLOW="renderer/js/feature-registry.js"
   139	          fi

   141

 function buildFixture() {
   const dir = H.acquireTempDir('r3-train-fix-');
   fs.mkdirSync(path.join(dir, 'governance'), { recursive: true });
   for (const sub of ['r3.0', 'r3.0c', 'r3.0d', 'r3.0e', 'r3.0f']) {
     fs.cpSync(path.join(REPO, 'governance', sub), path.join(dir, 'governance', sub), { recursive: true });
   }
   return dir;
 }
 
 function readJson(p) { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8')); }
 function writeJson(p, o) { fs.writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(o, null, 2)); }
 
 // ── 1. legal initial train (real repo) ──
 {
   const r = runValidator(null);
   chk('PASS real repo rc=0', r.status === 0, { violations: r.artifact && r.artifact.violations });
   chk('PASS artifact ok=true', r.artifact && r.artifact.ok === true);
-  chk('PASS trainStatus=IN_PROGRESS', r.artifact && r.artifact.trainStatus === 'IN_PROGRESS');
+  // F6: the Train PR (#16) is merged to main (0711e74a) — MERGED is the correct terminal
+  // status per train.schema.json trainStatusValues; IN_PROGRESS would now be stale.
+  chk('PASS trainStatus=MERGED', r.artifact && r.artifact.trainStatus === 'MERGED');
   chk('PASS currentPhase is a valid R3.0 phase', r.artifact && ['R3.0C', 'R3.0D', 'R3.0E', 'R3.0F'].indexOf(r.artifact.currentPhase) !== -1);
   chk('PASS targetVersion=2.0.0', r.artifact && r.artifact.targetVersion === '2.0.0');
   chk('PASS targetTag=v2.0.0', r.artifact && r.artifact.targetTag === 'v2.0.0');
   chk('PASS intermediateReleaseAllowed=false', r.artifact && r.artifact.intermediateReleaseAllowed === false);
   chk('PASS r4Excluded=true', r.artifact && r.artifact.r4Excluded === true);
   chk('PASS all 4 phases summarized', r.artifact && r.artifact.phases && Object.keys(r.artifact.phases).length === 4);
 }
 
 // ── 2a. D current=D2 but R3.0C still at C7_UI (pre-C8) → CROSS_PHASE_PREMATURE_ADVANCE ──
 // Note: the real-repo governance is at C8_ACTIVATION after activation; the fixture must roll the
 // R3.0C copy back to a pre-C8 checkpoint to recreate the "D advancing before C8 reached" premise.
 {
   const dir = buildFixture();
   const cs = readJson(path.join(dir, 'governance', 'r3.0c', 'state.json'));
   cs.currentCheckpoint = 'C7_UI'; cs.featureRegistryActivationAllowed = false;
   cs.enabledCapabilities = (cs.enabledCapabilities || []).filter(c => c !== 'feature_registry_active');

   writeJson(path.join(dir, 'governance', 'r3.0c', 'state.json'), cs);

 (function () {
   // R3.0C-specific assertions on the train state. After R3.0D D1 advances the train, train.currentPhase
   // moves to R3.0D and train.currentPhaseCheckpoint becomes the active R3.0D checkpoint. R3.0C state
   // however remains pinned at C8_ACTIVATION / finalActivationReached=true (R3.0C is complete). The
   // tests below assert what is INVARIANT — R3.0C is done — without false-failing when R3.0D advances.
   const train = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(REPO, 'governance', 'r3.0', 'train.json'), 'utf8'));
   const r3c = train.phaseStates && train.phaseStates['R3.0C'];
   chk('train.phaseStates.R3.0C.currentCheckpoint=C8_ACTIVATION', r3c && r3c.currentCheckpoint === 'C8_ACTIVATION');
   chk('train.phaseStates.R3.0C.finalActivationReached=true', r3c && r3c.finalActivationReached === true);
   chk('train.phaseStates.R3.0C.finalActivationCheckpoint=C8_ACTIVATION', r3c && r3c.finalActivationCheckpoint === 'C8_ACTIVATION');
   chk('train.currentPhase=R3.0C OR train has advanced to a later phase', train.currentPhase === 'R3.0C' || ['R3.0D', 'R3.0E', 'R3.0F'].indexOf(train.currentPhase) !== -1);
   // R3.0F MUST NOT have reached its final activation (F6_RELEASE) before R3.0C C8_ACTIVATION;
   // it MAY be started (R3.0F.started=true) once R3.0E E5_ACTIVATION finalActivationReached=true,
   // mirroring the precedent that allowed R3.0D to start after R3.0C C8 and R3.0E to start after
   // R3.0D D5. Per the SKYLINE-pinned scope (no intermediate release), F6_RELEASE itself is the
   // single boundary that authorizes the version bump and the post-merge tag.
   ['R3.0F'].forEach(function (p) {
     const s = train.phaseStates && train.phaseStates[p];
     chk('train.phaseStates.' + p + '.started is boolean', s && (s.started === true || s.started === false));
-    chk('train.phaseStates.' + p + '.finalActivationReached=false', s && s.finalActivationReached === false);
+    // Pre-F6 this had to be false (no premature final activation before C8). At F6_RELEASE
+    // the flag legitimately flips true — but ONLY together with currentCheckpoint=F6_RELEASE,
+    // so the anti-skip property this assertion has always protected stays intact.
+    chk('train.phaseStates.' + p + '.finalActivationReached consistent with its checkpoint',
+      s && (s.finalActivationReached === false
+        || (s.finalActivationReached === true && s.currentCheckpoint === 'F6_RELEASE')));
   });
   // R3.0E is started; after E5_ACTIVATION the finalActivationReached flag may be
   // either false (in-progress) or true (R3.0E phase complete). Both are valid Train
   // intermediate states. The R3.0F phase-state check below remains the source of
   // truth for whether the Integrated Delivery Train has reached its final stage.
   (function () {
     const s = train.phaseStates && train.phaseStates['R3.0E'];
     chk('train.phaseStates.R3.0E exists', s !== undefined);
     chk('train.phaseStates.R3.0E.finalActivationReached is boolean',
       s && (s.finalActivationReached === true || s.finalActivationReached === false));
   })();

    28	const REPO = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
    29	const SCRIPT = path.join(REPO, 'scripts', 'check-version-policy.js');
    30	
    31	// Sandbox: a minimal git repo so the script's git calls behave; its package.json is
    32	// the variable under test. The script resolves REPO relative to its own location, so
    33	// we copy the script into the sandbox's scripts/ dir to point it at the fixture.
    34	function runPolicy(version, env) {
    35	  const box = H.acquireTempDir('f6-version-policy-');
    36	  try {
    37	    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(box, 'scripts'), { recursive: true });
    38	    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(box, '.github', 'workflows'), { recursive: true });
    39	    fs.copyFileSync(SCRIPT, path.join(box, 'scripts', 'check-version-policy.js'));
    40	    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(box, 'package.json'), JSON.stringify({ name: 'fixture', version: version }) + '\n');
    41	    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(box, '.github', 'workflows', 'ci.yml'), 'name: x\non:\n  push:\n');
    42	    cp.execSync('git init -q && git add -A && git -c user.email=t@t -c user.name=t commit -qm x', { cwd: box });
    43	    const r = cp.spawnSync(process.execPath, [path.join(box, 'scripts', 'check-version-policy.js')], {
    44	      cwd: box, encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 30000,
    45	      env: Object.assign({}, process.env, { ARTIFACT_DIR: path.join(box, 'artifacts'), BASE_SHA: 'HEAD', HEAD_SHA: 'HEAD' }, env || {}),
    46	    });
    47	    let artifact = null;
    48	    try { artifact = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(box, 'artifacts', 'version-policy.json'), 'utf8')); } catch (_) { }
    49	    return { status: r.status, artifact: artifact };
    50	  } finally {
    51	    H.releaseTempDir(box);
    52	  }
    53	}

    55	// 1. The post-bump pin: 2.0.0 passes with no allow env.
    56	(function () {

    58	  chk('pin: version 2.0.0 passes with NO VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW', r.status === 0 && r.artifact && r.artifact.ok === true, r.artifact);
    59	})();

    63	  const r = runPolicy('2.0.1', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '' });
    64	  chk('fail-closed: 2.0.1 REJECTED without allow', r.status !== 0 && r.artifact && r.artifact.ok === false);
    65	  const r2 = runPolicy('2.1.0', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '' });
    66	  chk('fail-closed: 2.1.0 REJECTED without allow', r2.status !== 0);
    67	  const r3 = runPolicy('2.0.0-rc.1', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '' });
    68	  chk('fail-closed: 2.0.0-rc.1 REJECTED without allow', r3.status !== 0);
    69	})();
    70	
    71	// 3. No rollback: the PRE-bump value 1.4.0 now fails without an explicit allow.
    72	(function () {
    73	  const r = runPolicy('1.4.0', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '' });
    74	  chk('no-rollback: 1.4.0 REJECTED after the F6 pin moved to 2.0.0', r.status !== 0 && r.artifact && r.artifact.ok === false);
    75	})();
    76	
    77	// 4. Exact-allow-only: an allow admits exactly that version and nothing else.
    78	(function () {
    79	  const r = runPolicy('3.0.0', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '3.0.0' });
    80	  chk('allow: future authorized bump 3.0.0 passes ONLY with exact allow', r.status === 0);
    81	  const r2 = runPolicy('2.0.1', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '3.0.0' });
    82	  chk('allow: 2.0.1 still REJECTED when allow=3.0.0 (exact match only)', r2.status !== 0);
    83	  const r3 = runPolicy('3.0.1', { VERSION_BUMP_ALLOW: '3.0.0' });

    92	})();
